2026-10-12T06:58:42.4713787Z  	CREATE FUNCTION "journal_migration_aea4ba4c52354a619d9f3503a73bd0ec".capture_workflow_event_journal() RETURNS trigger
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4713981Z  	LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY INVOKER SET search_path=pg_catalog AS $$
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4714065Z  	DECLARE
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4714221Z  	 c jsonb; r jsonb; ledger_state text; i record; t record;
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4714428Z  	 wanted_task uuid; wanted_node uuid; wanted_target uuid; wanted_epoch bigint;
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4714522Z  	 wanted_source text;
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4714605Z  	BEGIN
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4714795Z  	 SELECT command_json,receipt_json,state INTO STRICT c,r,ledger_state
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4715115Z  	 FROM "journal_migration_aea4ba4c52354a619d9f3503a73bd0ec".workflow_commands WHERE command_id=NEW.command_id;
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4715370Z  	 SELECT wi.*, wv.version_id AS original_version_id, wd.name AS original_name INTO STRICT i
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4715600Z  	 FROM "journal_migration_aea4ba4c52354a619d9f3503a73bd0ec".workflow_instances wi
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4715990Z  	 JOIN "journal_migration_aea4ba4c52354a619d9f3503a73bd0ec".workflow_definitions wd ON wd.app_id=wi.app_id AND wd.id=wi.flow_id
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4716477Z  	 JOIN "journal_migration_aea4ba4c52354a619d9f3503a73bd0ec".workflow_versions wv ON wv.app_id=wi.app_id AND wv.flow_id=wi.flow_id AND wv.version=wi.definition_version
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4716631Z  	 WHERE wi.app_id=NEW.app_id AND wi.id=NEW.instance_id;
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4716781Z  	 IF (c->>'ProtocolVersion')::integer IS DISTINCT FROM 2
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4716931Z  	 OR (c->>'CommandID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.command_id
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4717055Z  	 OR (c->>'AppID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.app_id
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4717206Z  	 OR (c->>'InstanceID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.instance_id
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4717339Z  	 OR (c->>'ActorID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.actor_id
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4717459Z  	 OR c->>'Action' IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.action
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4717634Z  	 OR (c->>'SchemaVersion')::bigint IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.schema_version
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4717808Z  	 OR (c->>'RecordVersion')::bigint IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.record_version
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4717940Z  	 OR (c->>'TableID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM i.table_id
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4718062Z  	 OR (c->>'ViewID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM i.view_id
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4718309Z  	 OR (c->>'RecordID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM i.record_id
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4718432Z  	 OR (c->>'FlowID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM i.flow_id
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4718598Z  	 OR (c->>'VersionID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM i.original_version_id
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4718797Z  	 OR (c->>'DefinitionVersion')::bigint IS DISTINCT FROM i.definition_version
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4719149Z  	 OR NEW.sequence IS DISTINCT FROM (c->>'ExpectedSequence')::bigint + (CASE WHEN NEW.outcome='success' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) THEN
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4719366Z  	  RAISE EXCEPTION 'workflow journal identity mismatch' USING ERRCODE='23514';
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4719450Z  	 END IF;
2026-10-12T06:58:42.4719658Z  	 -- INSERT is inside the original projection callback, before terminal ledger
